狂犬病 延迟就诊和院前伤口处理的年龄相关模式 暴露后预防:2022-2025 年在中国杭州进行的一项回顾性医院研究

Age-related patterns in delayed presentation and pre-hospital wound management for rabies post-exposure prophylaxis: a retrospective hospital-based study in Hangzhou, China, 2022–2025
作者： Zheng Yang, Yunlong Zhu, Kaiqi Cao, Xianglong Yi, Siqi Tian, Bin Yan, Zhifang Zhao
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在潜在的 狂犬病 暴露后,及时的 狂犬病 (PEP) 和适当的伤口护理至关重要,而老年人可能面临这两者的障碍。
设计与对象
本研究描述了中国杭州一家 队列研究 医院的就诊时间和院前伤口处理,包括 2022 年 10 月至 2025 年 10 月期间排除各种不相关记录后的 3327 例与暴露相关的临床就诊。
核心方法
进行了一项回顾性横断面研究,使用单独的多变量逻辑模型评估就诊时间和任何院前伤口自我 治疗。
主要结果
218/3327 (6.6%) exposure-related presentations occurred on a subsequent calendar day. The clinic-opening calendar period (October–December 2022) was associated with subsequent-calendar-day presentation (AOR 2.54, 95% CI 1.63–3.96; P < 0.001), as was pre-hospital wound self-treatment (AOR 2.72, 95% CI 2.00–3.69; P < 0.001).
解释与创新
Older age was associated with subsequent-calendar-day presentation, with patients aged ≥ 65 years having higher adjusted odds than those aged 15–44 years (AOR 2.28, 95% CI 1.31–3.96; P = 0.003).
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施来提高老年人的 狂犬病 PEP 依从性,特别是在诊所运营的早期。
局限与证据强度
与诊所开业日历期间的关联应被解释为探索性的,可能反映诊所的建立、病例组合、季节性或未测量的访问因素。

Age-related patterns in delayed presentation and pre-hospital wound management for rabies post-exposure prophylaxis: a retrospective hospital-based study in Hangzhou, China, 2022–2025

Authors: Zheng Yang, Yunlong Zhu, Kaiqi Cao, Xianglong Yi, Siqi Tian, Bin Yan, Zhifang Zhao
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Timely rabies post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) and appropriate wound care are essential after potential rabies exposure, and older adults may face barriers to both. This study described presentation timing and pre-hospital wound management in a hospital-based cohort in Hangzhou, China. Methods This retrospective cross-sectional study included 3327 exposure-related clinic presentations between October 2022 and October 2025 after exclusion of 7 system-test entries, 15 WHO Category I exposures, 13 confirmed continuation visits, 10 contradictory-date records and 2 unresolved duplicate records. Subsequent-calendar-day presentation was a date-based operational proxy for delay. Separate multivariable logistic models evaluated presentation timing and any pre-hospital wound self-treatment. Results 218/3327 (6.6%) exposure-related presentations occurred on a subsequent calendar day. The clinic-opening calendar period (October–December 2022) was associated with subsequent-calendar-day presentation (AOR 2.54, 95% CI 1.63–3.96; P < 0.001), as was pre-hospital wound self-treatment (AOR 2.72, 95% CI 2.00–3.69; P < 0.001). Patients aged ≥ 65 years had higher adjusted odds of subsequent-calendar-day presentation than those aged 15–44 years (AOR 2.28, 95% CI 1.31–3.96; P = 0.003). The estimate for any self-treatment in the ≥ 65-year group was lower but did not reach the conventional significance threshold (AOR 0.71, 95% CI 0.50–1.01; P = 0.056). Conclusions In this hospital-based cohort, older age was associated with subsequent-calendar-day presentation. The estimate for lower self-treatment among older adults was imprecise and requires confirmation; the two outcomes were modelled separately and do not establish a combined individual-level outcome. Associations with the clinic-opening calendar period should be interpreted as exploratory because they may reflect clinic establishment, case mix, seasonality, or unmeasured access factors. Trial registration Clinical trial number: Not applicable.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Timely rabies post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) and appropriate wound care are essential after potential rabies exposure, and older adults may face barriers to both.
Design and population
This study described presentation timing and pre-hospital wound management in a hospital-based cohort in Hangzhou, China, including 3327 exposure-related clinic presentations between October 2022 and October 2025 after excluding various non-relevant records.
Core methods
A retrospective cross-sectional study was conducted, with separate multivariable logistic models evaluating presentation timing and any pre-hospital wound self-treatment.
Main results
218/3327 (6.6%) exposure-related presentations occurred on a subsequent calendar day. The clinic-opening calendar period (October–December 2022) was associated with subsequent-calendar-day presentation (AOR 2.54, 95% CI 1.63–3.96; P < 0.001), as was pre-hospital wound self-treatment (AOR 2.72, 95% CI 2.00–3.69; P < 0.001).
Interpretation and novelty
Older age was associated with subsequent-calendar-day presentation, with patients aged ≥ 65 years having higher adjusted odds than those aged 15–44 years (AOR 2.28, 95% CI 1.31–3.96; P = 0.003).
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings highlight the need for targeted interventions to improve rabies PEP adherence in older adults, particularly in the early period of clinic operation.
Limitations and evidence strength
Associations with the clinic-opening calendar period should be interpreted as exploratory, potentially reflecting clinic establishment, case mix, seasonality, or unmeasured access factors.

靶向 狂犬病 糖蛋白 G 的全长 mRNA 疫苗 的免疫信息学设计和结构评估

Immunoinformatic Design and Structural Evaluation of a Full-Length mRNA Vaccine Targeting Rabies Glycoprotein G
作者： Islam B. Abouzid, Mai M. Lotfy, Ahmed Ragab Elbestawy
摘要中文翻译背景/目标:狂犬病 仍然是一种致命的病毒性疾病,当前传统的 疫苗 策略在结构稳定性和大规模制造方面面临重大挑战。本研究旨在使用集成的免疫信息学和结构建模框架设计一种针对 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白 G (RABV-G) 的新型信使核糖核酸 (mRNA) 疫苗 候选物。方法:系统评估 RABV-G 序列的进化保守性和安全性,然后进行 3D 结构建模。基于人类白细胞抗原 (HLA) 结合、毒性和糖基化屏蔽分析,鉴定了高抗原性 B 细胞和 T 细胞表位。通过与 Toll 样受体 4/骨髓分化因子 2 (TLR4-MD2) 复合物的分子对接来评估功能相互作用。最后,设计了全长 mRNA 构建体,并通过计算评估了翻译效率、结构稳定性和免疫模拟。结果:RABV-G 靶标表现出高度的进化保守性,在 87 个分析序列中,53.82% 的位置完全相同,另外 43.70% 的位置高度保守 (70-99%)。结构验证证实了高质量模型,能够提取可接近的非糖基化表位。分子对接模拟揭示了与 TLR4-MD2 受体的高度有利的预测结构相互作用,表明其具有参与先天免疫途径的潜在结构能力,有待实验验证。密码子优化的 mRNA 构建体表现出预测的热力学稳定二级结构 (MFE = -429.50 kcal/mol) 和可访问的翻译起始位点,有待实验验证。此外,计算机免疫模拟预测了体液免疫和细胞免疫的强劲且持续的激活,特别是记忆 B 细胞的扩增。结论:计算设计的 mRNA 疫苗 候选物表现出非常有利的结构特征和免疫原性潜力。虽然这些计算机研究结果为 狂犬病 预防提供了一个有前途的基础框架,但严格需要随后的实验实验室验证来确认其功能功效和翻译。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 仍然是一种致命的病毒性疾病,当前传统的 疫苗 策略在结构稳定性和大规模制造方面面临着重大挑战。本研究旨在使用集成的免疫信息学和结构建模框架设计一种针对 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白 G (RABV-G) 的新型信使核糖核酸 (mRNA) 疫苗 候选物。
设计与对象
该研究采用计算机模拟方法进行,重点关注 狂犬病病毒 糖蛋白 G (RABV-G) 及其结构和免疫原性特性。该研究涉及对 87 个 RABV-G 分析序列进行评估,以评估进化保守性和安全性。
核心方法
对 RABV-G 序列的进化保守性和安全性进行了系统评估,然后进行 3D 结构建模。基于人类白细胞抗原 (HLA) 结合、毒性和糖基化屏蔽分析,鉴定了高抗原性 B 细胞和 T 细胞表位。通过与 Toll 样受体 4/骨髓分化因子 2 (TLR4-MD2) 复合物的分子对接来评估功能相互作用。设计了全长 mRNA 构建体,并通过计算评估了翻译效率、结构稳定性和免疫模拟。
主要结果
RABV-G 靶标表现出高度的进化保守性,在 87 个分析序列中,53.82% 的位置完全相同,另外 43.70% 的位置高度保守 (70-99%)。结构验证证实了高质量模型,能够提取可接近的非糖基化表位。分子对接模拟揭示了与 TLR4-MD2 受体的高度有利的预测结构相互作用,表明其具有参与先天免疫途径的潜在结构能力,有待实验验证。密码子优化的 mRNA 构建体表现出预测的热力学稳定二级结构 (MFE = -429.50 kcal/mol) 和可访问的翻译起始位点,有待实验验证。
解释与创新
计算设计的 mRNA 疫苗 候选物表现出非常有利的结构特征和免疫原性潜力。这些计算机研究结果为 狂犬病 预防提供了一个有前途的基础框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为开发针对 狂犬病 的新 mRNA 疫苗 提供了有希望的基础,这可以提高结构稳定性和大规模生产。该结果可能为未来 疫苗 设计和免疫原性的研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于计算机分析,需要随后的实验实验室验证以确认功能功效和翻译。证据是温和的,因为它依赖于计算模型而不是经验数据。

Immunoinformatic Design and Structural Evaluation of a Full-Length mRNA Vaccine Targeting Rabies Glycoprotein G

Authors: Islam B. Abouzid, Mai M. Lotfy, Ahmed Ragab Elbestawy
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Rabies remains a fatal viral disease, and current conventional vaccine strategies face significant challenges regarding structural stability and large-scale manufacturing. This study aimed to design a novel messenger ribonucleic acid (mRNA) vaccine candidate targeting the rabies virus glycoprotein G (RABV-G) using an integrated immunoinformatic and structural modeling framework. Methods: The RABV-G sequence was systematically evaluated for evolutionary conservation and safety, followed by 3D structural modeling. Highly antigenic B-cell and T-cell epitopes were identified based on human leukocyte antigen (HLA) binding, toxicity, and glycosylation shielding analyses. Functional interactions were assessed via molecular docking with the Toll-like Receptor 4/Myeloid Differentiation factor 2 (TLR4-MD2) complex. Finally, a full-length mRNA construct was engineered and computationally evaluated for translational efficiency, structural stability, and immune simulation. Results: The RABV-G target exhibited high evolutionary conservation, with 53.82% of positions fully identical and an additional 43.70% highly conserved (70–99%) across 87 analyzed sequences. Structural validation confirmed a high-quality model, enabling the extraction of accessible, non-glycosylated epitopes. Molecular docking simulations revealed a highly favorable predicted structural interaction with the TLR4-MD2 receptor, suggesting a potential structural capacity to engage innate immune pathways, pending experimental validation. The codon-optimized mRNA construct exhibited a predicted thermodynamically stable secondary structure (MFE = −429.50 kcal/mol) with an accessible translation initiation site, pending experimental validation. Furthermore, in silico immune simulations predicted a robust and sustained activation of both humoral and cellular immunity, notably including memory B-cell expansion. Conclusions: The computationally designed mRNA vaccine candidate demonstrates highly favorable structural characteristics and immunogenic potential. While these in silico findings present a promising foundational framework for rabies prevention, subsequent experimental laboratory validation is strictly required to confirm its functional efficacy and translation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies remains a fatal viral disease, and current conventional vaccine strategies face significant challenges regarding structural stability and large-scale manufacturing. This study aimed to design a novel messenger ribonucleic acid (mRNA) vaccine candidate targeting the rabies virus glycoprotein G (RABV-G) using an integrated immunoinformatic and structural modeling framework.
Design and population
The study was conducted using an in silico approach, focusing on the rabies virus glycoprotein G (RABV-G) and its structural and immunogenic properties. The research involved the evaluation of 87 analyzed sequences of RABV-G to assess evolutionary conservation and safety.
Core methods
The RABV-G sequence was systematically evaluated for evolutionary conservation and safety, followed by 3D structural modeling. Highly antigenic B-cell and T-cell epitopes were identified based on human leukocyte antigen (HLA) binding, toxicity, and glycosylation shielding analyses. Functional interactions were assessed via molecular docking with the Toll-like Receptor 4/Myeloid Differentiation factor 2 (TLR4-MD2) complex. A full-length mRNA construct was engineered and computationally evaluated for translational efficiency, structural stability, and immune simulation.
Main results
The RABV-G target exhibited high evolutionary conservation, with 53.82% of positions fully identical and an additional 43.70% highly conserved (70–99%) across 87 analyzed sequences. Structural validation confirmed a high-quality model, enabling the extraction of accessible, non-glycosylated epitopes. Molecular docking simulations revealed a highly favorable predicted structural interaction with the TLR4-MD2 receptor, suggesting a potential structural capacity to engage innate immune pathways, pending experimental validation. The codon-optimized mRNA construct exhibited a predicted thermodynamically stable secondary structure (MFE = −429.50 kcal/mol) with an accessible translation initiation site, pending experimental validation.
Interpretation and novelty
The computationally designed mRNA vaccine candidate demonstrates highly favorable structural characteristics and immunogenic potential. These in silico findings present a promising foundational framework for rabies prevention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a promising foundation for the development of a new mRNA vaccine against rabies, which could improve structural stability and large-scale manufacturing. The results may inform future studies on vaccine design and immunogenicity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on in silico analyses, which require subsequent experimental laboratory validation to confirm functional efficacy and translation. The evidence is moderate as it relies on computational models rather than empirical data.

Rabies Prevention in Practice, Awareness, Immediate Bite Response, Care-Seeking, and Barriers among Grade 5 Pupils and Their Parents in a Selected Public Elementary School in San Pablo City, Laguna

Rabies Prevention in Practice, Awareness, Immediate Bite Response, Care-Seeking, and Barriers among Grade 5 Pupils and Their Parents in a Selected Public Elementary School in San Pablo City, Laguna
作者： Gian Elson Paulus Gondra, Bressei Phenella Dela Cruz, Cienelle Louise Ligaya, Carlos Maria Miguel Tolentino, Kathleen B. Corcolon, Marc Lester F. Quintana
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 仍然是菲律宾的一个重要公共卫生问题,特别是对于学龄儿童来说,他们因频繁接触家养动物和流浪动物而面临更大的风险。
设计与对象
This study assessed the awareness, immediate bite response, care-seeking behavior, and perceived barriers to rabies prevention among Grade 5 pupils and their parents in San Pablo City, Laguna.
核心方法
使用结构化调查问卷收集数据,调查参与者对 狂犬病 传播 的了解、预防实践、即时伤口处理、寻求护理的意图以及 治疗 的障碍。
主要结果
调查结果显示,大多数参与者对狂犬病表现出较高的认知度,71.19%的学生和75.83%的家长认为狗咬伤是传播的主要形式。
解释与创新
然而,对于看似健康的动物仍然可能传播 狂犬病 的误解仍然存在,特别是在学生中。
科研与公卫意义
尽管大多数受访者表达了在动物咬伤后寻求医疗护理的强烈意愿,其中 90% 的学生和 85% 的家长表示他们将前往医疗机构就诊,但建议的急救措施仍然存在缺陷,包括适当的伤口清洗和避免传统疗法。
局限与证据强度
60% 的家长表示,经济困难、对 疫苗接种 的恐惧以及获得医疗设施的机会有限被认为是及时获得 治疗 的主要障碍。

Rabies Prevention in Practice, Awareness, Immediate Bite Response, Care-Seeking, and Barriers among Grade 5 Pupils and Their Parents in a Selected Public Elementary School in San Pablo City, Laguna

Authors: Gian Elson Paulus Gondra, Bressei Phenella Dela Cruz, Cienelle Louise Ligaya, Carlos Maria Miguel Tolentino, Kathleen B. Corcolon, Marc Lester F. Quintana
Original abstract (English)
Rabies remains a critical public health concern in the Philippines, particularly among school-aged children who are at increased risk because of frequent contact with domestic and stray animals. This study assessed the awareness, immediate bite response, care-seeking behavior, and perceived barriers to rabies prevention among Grade 5 pupils and their parents in San Pablo City, Laguna. A descriptive, comparative, and cross-sectional research design was employed, involving 302 Grade 5 pupils and their corresponding parents. Data were gathered using a structured questionnaire that examined participants’ knowledge of rabies transmission, prevention practices, immediate wound management, care-seeking intentions, and barriers to treatment. Findings revealed that most participants demonstrated a high level of awareness regarding rabies, with 71.19% of pupils and 75.83% of parents recognizing dog bites as the primary mode of transmission. However, misconceptions persisted, particularly among pupils, regarding the possibility that apparently healthy animals could still transmit rabies. Although most respondents expressed a strong intention to seek medical care following an animal bite, with 90% of pupils and 85% of parents indicating they would visit a health facility, deficiencies remained in recommended first-aid practices, including proper wound washing and avoidance of traditional remedies. Financial constraints, reported by 60% of parents, fear of vaccination, and limited access to healthcare facilities were identified as the major barriers to timely treatment. The findings indicate that awareness alone is insufficient to ensure appropriate preventive behaviors. Strengthening school- and community-based health education, correcting misconceptions, improving access to affordable post-exposure prophylaxis, and promoting collaboration among healthcare providers, educational institutions, and local government units are essential to enhance rabies prevention and encourage timely responses to potential rabies exposure.

Rabies remains a critical public health concern in the Philippines, particularly among school-aged children who are at increased risk because of frequent contact with domestic and stray animals.

Main results
Findings revealed that most participants demonstrated a high level of awareness regarding rabies, with 71.19% of pupils and 75.83% of parents recognizing dog bites as the primary mode of transmission.
Interpretation and novelty
However, misconceptions persisted, particularly among pupils, regarding the possibility that apparently healthy animals could still transmit rabies.
Scientific and public-health significance
Although most respondents expressed a strong intention to seek medical care following an animal bite, with 90% of pupils and 85% of parents indicating they would visit a health facility, deficiencies remained in recommended first-aid practices, including proper wound washing and avoidance of traditional remedies.
Limitations and evidence strength
Financial constraints, reported by 60% of parents, fear of vaccination, and limited access to healthcare facilities were identified as the major barriers to timely treatment.

树皮之下:绘制狗咬伤模式和伤害严重程度——卡纳塔克邦比达尔乡村健康培训中心桑斯布尔的一项横断面研究

Beneath the bark: mapping dog bite patterns and injury severity – a cross-sectional study at rural health training centre Santhpur, Bidar, Karnataka
作者： Deva C., Dilip S. Rathod, Pallavi M. Kesari
摘要中文翻译背景:狂犬病 是一种 疫苗 可预防的人畜共患病毒性疾病,总是影响中枢神经系统,在高达 99% 的人类病例中,狗是 传播 病毒的携带者。印度仍然是 狂犬病 的流行病,约占全球 狂犬病 死亡人数的 36%。目的是描述向农村卫生培训中心提出狗咬伤投诉的患者受伤的模式和严重程度。方法:2026 年 2 月至 2026 年 5 月在 Bidar 的 RHTC Santhpur 进行了一项前瞻性横断面研究。所有 63 名有狗咬伤投诉的患者均被纳入研究,并使用结构化调查问卷收集数据,内容涵盖社会人口统计资料、咬伤特征、伤口分类和暴露后管理。使用 Microsoft Excel 2022 进行分析。结果:63 名患者中,46 名 (73%) 为男性。受影响最大的年龄组是6-25岁(42.86%)。大多数咬伤事件发生在晚上(38.1%),其中流浪狗咬伤事件占(52.4%)。无端咬伤占大多数(77.78%)。下肢是最常被咬伤的部位(71.4%)。II 类伤口占主导地位(85.7%),III 类伤口也占多数(14.3%)。(98.4%) 的受害者使用肥皂和水进行急救。然而,只有(11.1%)的 III 类患者接受了 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白(RIG)。结论:狗咬伤主要影响年轻、生育年龄的男性,流浪狗和宠物狗的咬伤负担几乎相同。III 类患者 RIG 可用性的严重差距代表了一个严重的公共卫生问题,需要紧急系统干预。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
狂犬病 是一种 疫苗 可预防的人畜共患病毒性疾病,总是影响中枢神经系统,在高达 99% 的人类病例中,狗是 传播 病毒的传播者。印度仍然是 狂犬病 的流行病,约占全球 狂犬病 死亡人数的 36%。
设计与对象
2026 年 2 月至 2026 年 5 月在 Bidar 的 RHTC Santhpur 进行了一项前瞻性横断面研究。所有 63 名有狗咬伤投诉的患者均被纳入研究,并使用结构化调查问卷收集数据,内容涵盖社会人口统计资料、咬伤特征、伤口分类和暴露后管理。
核心方法
使用 Microsoft Excel 2022 进行分析。该研究旨在描述向农村卫生培训中心提出狗咬伤投诉的患者的受伤模式和严重程度。
主要结果
63 名患者中,46 名(73%)为男性。受影响最大的年龄组是6-25岁(42.86%)。大多数咬伤事件发生在晚上(38.1%),其中流浪狗咬伤事件占(52.4%)。无端咬伤占大多数(77.78%)。下肢是最常被咬伤的部位(71.4%)。II 类伤口占主导地位(85.7%),III 类伤口也占多数(14.3%)。(98.4%) 的受害者使用肥皂和水进行急救。然而,只有(11.1%)的 III 类患者接受了 狂犬病 免疫球蛋白(RIG)。
解释与创新
狗咬伤主要影响年轻的生育年龄的男性,流浪狗和宠物狗的咬伤负担几乎相同。III 类患者 RIG 可用性的严重差距代表了一个严重的公共卫生问题,需要紧急系统干预。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调需要改进暴露后管理,特别是对于 III 类伤口,并强调解决农村地区 RIG 可用性的公共卫生问题的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个农村卫生培训中心,可能无法推广到其他地区。证据是中等的,因为它是基于样本量相对较小的横断面研究。

Beneath the bark: mapping dog bite patterns and injury severity – a cross-sectional study at rural health training centre Santhpur, Bidar, Karnataka

Authors: Deva C., Dilip S. Rathod, Pallavi M. Kesari
Original abstract (English)
Background: Rabies is a vaccine-preventable, zoonotic, viral disease that invariably affects the central nervous system, with dogs responsible for virus transmission in up to 99% of human cases. India remains endemic for rabies and accounts for approximately 36% of global rabies deaths. Objectives were to describe the pattern and severity of injuries among patients presenting with dog bite complaints to rural health training centre. Methods: A prospective cross-sectional study was conducted from February 2026 to May 2026 at RHTC Santhpur, Bidar. All 63 patients presenting with dog bite complaints were enrolled and data were collected using a structured questionnaire covering socio-demographic profiles, bite characteristics, wound categorization, and post-exposure management. Analysis was performed using Microsoft Excel 2022. Results: Of the 63 patients, 46 (73%) were male. The most affected age group was 6-25 years (42.86%). The majority of bites occurred in the evening (38.1%) and stray dogs accounted for (52.4%) of bites. Unprovoked bites were predominant (77.78%). Lower limbs were the most commonly bitten site (71.4%). Category II wounds predominated (85.7%), while category III wounds constituted (14.3%). First aid with soap and water was performed by (98.4%) of victims. However, only (11.1%) of category III patients received rabies immunoglobulin (RIG). Conclusions: Dog bites predominantly affect young, productive-age males, with a near-equal burden from stray and pet dogs. The critical gap in RIG availability for category III patients represents a serious public health concern requiring urgent systemic intervention.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Rabies is a vaccine-preventable, zoonotic, viral disease that invariably affects the central nervous system, with dogs responsible for virus transmission in up to 99% of human cases. India remains endemic for rabies and accounts for approximately 36% of global rabies deaths.
Design and population
A prospective cross-sectional study was conducted from February 2026 to May 2026 at RHTC Santhpur, Bidar. All 63 patients presenting with dog bite complaints were enrolled and data were collected using a structured questionnaire covering socio-demographic profiles, bite characteristics, wound categorization, and post-exposure management.
Core methods
Analysis was performed using Microsoft Excel 2022. The study aimed to describe the pattern and severity of injuries among patients presenting with dog bite complaints to rural health training centre.
Main results
Of the 63 patients, 46 (73%) were male. The most affected age group was 6-25 years (42.86%). The majority of bites occurred in the evening (38.1%) and stray dogs accounted for (52.4%) of bites. Unprovoked bites were predominant (77.78%). Lower limbs were the most commonly bitten site (71.4%). Category II wounds predominated (85.7%), while category III wounds constituted (14.3%). First aid with soap and water was performed by (98.4%) of victims. However, only (11.1%) of category III patients received rabies immunoglobulin (RIG).
Interpretation and novelty
Dog bites predominantly affect young, productive-age males, with a near-equal burden from stray and pet dogs. The critical gap in RIG availability for category III patients represents a serious public health concern requiring urgent systemic intervention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for improved post-exposure management, particularly for category III wounds, and underscore the importance of addressing the public health concern of RIG availability in rural settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single rural health training centre and may not be generalizable to other regions. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a cross-sectional study with a relatively small sample size.

泰国社区狗管理和 狂犬病 意识的混合方法研究

A mixed-methods study of community dog management and rabies awareness in Thailand
作者： Weerakorn Thichumpa, Anuwat Wiratsudakul, Wirichada Pan-ngum, Thomas Rawson, Katharina D. Hauck, Tini Garske, Wirichada Pan Ngum
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 的消除需要跨人类与动物环境界面的协调干预,但狗群管理、社区参与和治理之间的系统性差距往往阻碍持续进展。本研究调查了泰国高流行地区与 狂犬病 预防和控制相关的社区看法和挑战。采用混合方法设计,将省级二级定量数据与高流行地区 57 个利益相关者(包括公共卫生官员、政策制定者、非政府组织、动物福利联络员和社区成员)的定性主题分析相结合。调查结果表明标准的 暴露后预防 意识,98.2% 的参与者表现出良好的预防实践,认为 暴露后预防 (PEP) 至关重要,68.4% 的参与者拥有良好的知识。尽管大多数人支持狗绝育(59.6%)和疫苗接种(63.2%),但只有19.3%的人认为现有的控制活动有效。主要挑战包括运营资源有限(57.9%)和地方政府预算有限(40.4%)。社区狗自由漫步的情况很普遍,54.4% 的参与者认为遗弃是狗数量增长的主要驱动力。非正式喂养网络为社区狗提供本地化护理。而公众对基金管理的意愿相对较低(33.3%愿意支付;19.3%支持共同支付),反映了对地方当局责任的期望。从质量上看,公共卫生指令和动物福利法律框架之间的界限不明确,增加了 狂犬病 狂犬病 暴发 应对措施的复杂性,有时还会给地方政府和非政府部门带来制度性挑战。尽管如此,利益攸关方强烈支持持续绝育和部门间合作。解决政策与实践之间的差距、稳定融资以及将社区社会动态纳入正式的“同一个健康”治理对于实现可持续消除 狂犬病 至关重要。

A mixed-methods study of community dog management and rabies awareness in Thailand

Authors: Weerakorn Thichumpa, Anuwat Wiratsudakul, Wirichada Pan-ngum, Thomas Rawson, Katharina D. Hauck, Tini Garske, Wirichada Pan Ngum
Original abstract (English)
Rabies elimination requires coordinated interventions across the human-animal-environment interface, yet sustained progress is often hindered by systemic gaps between dog population management, community engagement, and governance. This study investigated community perceptions and challenges related to rabies prevention and control across high-endemic areas in Thailand. Using a mixed-methods design, secondary provincial quantitative data were integrated with qualitative thematic analysis from 57 stakeholders in high endemic areas, including public health officials, policymakers, NGOs, animal-welfare liaisons, and community members. The findings indicated standard rabies awareness, 98.2% of participants demonstrated good preventive practices, recognized post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) as essential, and 68.4% possessed good knowledge. Although most supported dog sterilization (59.6%) and vaccination (63.2%), only 19.3% perceived existing control campaigns as effective. Main challenges included constrained operational resources (57.9%) and limited local government budgets (40.4%). Free-roaming community dogs were prevalent, with 54.4% of participants identifying abandonment as the primary driver of population growth. Informal feeding networks provided localized care for community dogs. While public willingness to fund management was relatively low (33.3% willing to pay; 19.3% supporting co-payment), reflecting an expectation of local authority responsibility. Qualitatively, unclear boundaries between public health mandates and animal welfare legal frameworks added complexity to rabies outbreak response and sometimes introduced institutional challenges among local government and non-governmental sectors. Nevertheless, stakeholders strongly supported sustained sterilization and intersectoral collaboration. Addressing policy-practice gaps, stabilizing financing, and integrating community social dynamics into formal One Health governance are crucial for achieving sustainable rabies elimination.

Question and background
Rabies elimination requires coordinated interventions across the human-animal-environment interface, yet sustained progress is often hindered by systemic gaps between dog population management, community engagement, and governance.
Design and population
This study investigated community perceptions and challenges related to rabies prevention and control across high-endemic areas in Thailand.
Core methods
Using a mixed-methods design, secondary provincial quantitative data were integrated with qualitative thematic analysis from 57 stakeholders in high endemic areas, including public health officials, policymakers, NGOs, animal-welfare liaisons, and community members.
Main results
The findings indicated standard rabies awareness, 98.2% of participants demonstrated good preventive practices, recognized post-exposure prophylaxis (PEP) as essential, and 68.4% possessed good knowledge.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessment.

外来鹿(Cervus timorensis)与羊相关的恶性卡他热:对巴西南部野生动物健康的影响

Sheep-Associated Malignant Catarrhal Fever in an Exotic Rusa Deer (Cervus timorensis): Implications for Wildlife Health in Southern Brazil
作者： Selwyn Arlington Headley, Maria Constanza Rodriguez, Fernanda Pinto Ferreira, Alais Maria Dall Agnol, Flávia Helena Pereira Silva, Geovana Depieri Yoshitani, Amauri Alcindo Alfieri, Renato Silva de Sousa
摘要中文翻译羊相关恶性卡他热 (SA-MCF) 是一种由羊伽马疱疹病毒 2 (OvGHV2) 引起的常见致命性、全嗜性、淋巴组织增生性疾病,其中羊为无症状 储存宿主 宿主。尽管已知鲁萨鹿 (Cervus timorensis) 在其他地区对 OvGHV2 高度敏感,但巴西鹿尚未有病例记录。本报告描述了来自巴西南部的圈养鲁萨鹿中自发发生的 OvGHV2 感染,并讨论了对野生动物可能产生的影响。来自同一农场的三只鹿在没有事先临床症状的情况下突然死亡。这些动物与绵羊或山羊没有已知的接触,但被维持在一个流行病学生态位中,在亚临床感染的自由放养野猪 (FRWB) 和牛中检测到 OvGHV2。其中一份已提交用于例行尸检评估。肉眼病变包括消化道溃疡性和糜烂性病变、肺水肿和明显的脑膜充血。组织病理学显示糜烂性和溃疡性食管炎、皱胃炎、小肠炎和瘤胃炎,伴有非化脓性脑膜脑炎和多个器官的血管炎,与 SA-MCF 一致。通过 qPCR 对脑组织进行分子检测,检测到 OvGHV2 DNA。此外,PCR 检测扩增了 OvGHV2 被膜蛋白 (ORF75) 和糖蛋白 (gB) 基因的部分片段。未检测到反刍动物的其他神经病原体,包括 BoAHV5、BVDV、单核细胞增生李斯特菌、睡眠嗜组织菌和 狂犬病病毒。此外,基于 ORF75 和 gB 基因的系统发育分析表明,来自本研究的核苷酸序列与 OvGHV2 参考菌株和来自不同地理位置的反刍动物的其他菌株聚集在一起,并具有较高的序列同源性。由于没有与绵羊接触,再加上同一流行病学生态位内的 FRWB 和牛存在亚临床 OvGHV2 感染的先前证据,表明病毒 传播 中涉及替代野生动物 储存宿主 或桥梁 宿主。此外,这些结果扩大了巴西 OvGHV2 的 宿主 范围,突出了外来鹿科动物对 SA-MCF 的脆弱性,并强调在多个易感物种共存的地区需要采取综合野生动物 - 牲畜 监测 策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查巴西南部鲁萨鹿 (Cervus timorensis) 中羊相关恶性卡他热 (SA-MCF) 的发生情况,以解决该地区有关该物种对 OvGHV2 易感性的知识空白。
设计与对象
该研究对三只鲁萨鹿进行了回顾性分析,这些鲁萨鹿在没有事先临床症状的情况下突然死亡,这些鹿来自巴西南部的一处房产,在那里,在亚临床感染的自由放养的野猪和牛中检测到了 OvGHV2。
核心方法
该研究利用组织病理学、分子检测(qPCR 和 PCR)和系统发育分析来确认鲁萨鹿体内的 OvGHV2 感染。
主要结果
组织病理学和分子测试证实了三只鲁萨鹿之一的 OvGHV2 感染,系统发育分析显示与参考 OvGHV2 菌株的序列同源性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,替代野生动物 储存宿主 或桥梁 宿主 参与了病毒 传播,扩大了 OvGHV2 在巴西的 宿主 范围,并强调了外来鹿科动物对 SA-MCF 的脆弱性。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调,需要在多种易感物种共存的地区采取综合野生动物-牲畜 监测 策略,以防止 OvGHV2 的传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计以及鹿和羊之间缺乏直接接触证据而受到限制,但同一流行病学生态位中其他野生动物物种中存在 OvGHV2 支持了该研究结果。

Sheep-Associated Malignant Catarrhal Fever in an Exotic Rusa Deer (Cervus timorensis): Implications for Wildlife Health in Southern Brazil

Authors: Selwyn Arlington Headley, Maria Constanza Rodriguez, Fernanda Pinto Ferreira, Alais Maria Dall Agnol, Flávia Helena Pereira Silva, Geovana Depieri Yoshitani, Amauri Alcindo Alfieri, Renato Silva de Sousa
Original abstract (English)
Sheep-associated malignant catarrhal fever (SA-MCF) is a frequently fatal, pantropic, lymphoproliferative disease caused by ovine gammaherpesvirus 2 (OvGHV2), with sheep serving as the asymptomatic reservoir host. Although the Rusa deer (Cervus timorensis) are known to be highly susceptible to OvGHV2 in other regions, cases have not been documented in this species from Brazil. This report describes the spontaneous occurrence of OvGHV2 infection in a captive Rusa deer from Southern Brazil and discusses possible impacts on wildlife. Three deer from the same property died suddenly without prior clinical signs. These animals had no known contact with sheep or goats, but were maintained in an epidemiological niche where OvGHV2 was detected in subclinically infected free-ranging wild boars (FRWBs) and cattle. One of these was submitted for routine post-mortem evaluations. Gross lesions included ulcerative and erosive lesions of the alimentary tract, pulmonary edema, and marked meningeal congestion. Histopathology revealed erosive and ulcerative esophagitis, abomasitis, omasitis, and rumenitis, with nonsuppurative meningoencephalitis, and vasculitis in several organs, consistent with SA-MCF. Molecular testing of brain tissue detected OvGHV2 DNA by qPCR. Furthermore, PCR assays amplified the partial fragments of the OvGHV2 tegument protein (ORF75) and the glycoprotein (gB) genes. Other neurological pathogens of ruminants including BoAHV5, BVDV, Listeria monocytogenes, Histophilus somni, and rabies virus were not detected. Additionally, phylogenetic analyses, based on ORF75 and gB genes, revealed that the nucleotide sequences derived from this study clustered with and had elevated sequence homology with the OvGHV2 reference strains and other strains derived from ruminants maintained in different geographical locations. The absence of contact with sheep, combined with prior evidence of subclinical OvGHV2 infection in FRWBs and cattle within the same epidemiological niche, suggests the involvement of alternative wildlife reservoirs or bridge hosts in viral transmission. Additionally, these results expand the host range of OvGHV2 in Brazil, highlight the vulnerability of exotic cervids to SA-MCF, and underscore the need for integrated wildlife–livestock surveillance strategies in regions where multiple susceptible species coexist.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the occurrence of sheep-associated malignant catarrhal fever (SA-MCF) in Rusa deer (Cervus timorensis) in Southern Brazil, addressing the knowledge gap regarding the susceptibility of this species to OvGHV2 in the region.
Design and population
The study involved a retrospective analysis of three Rusa deer that died suddenly without prior clinical signs, from a property in Southern Brazil, where OvGHV2 was detected in subclinically infected free-ranging wild boars and cattle.
Core methods
The study utilized histopathology, molecular testing (qPCR and PCR), and phylogenetic analysis to confirm OvGHV2 infection in the Rusa deer.
Main results
Histopathology and molecular testing confirmed OvGHV2 infection in one of the three Rusa deer, with phylogenetic analysis showing sequence homology with reference OvGHV2 strains.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest the involvement of alternative wildlife reservoirs or bridge hosts in viral transmission, expanding the host range of OvGHV2 in Brazil and highlighting the vulnerability of exotic cervids to SA-MCF.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results underscore the need for integrated wildlife–livestock surveillance strategies in regions where multiple susceptible species coexist, to prevent the spread of OvGHV2.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and the absence of direct contact evidence between the deer and sheep, but the findings are supported by the presence of OvGHV2 in other wildlife species in the same epidemiological niche.

mRNA 构建体中牛 β-珠蛋白的 5' 非翻译区 (5' UTR) 可翻译不同类型的抗原蛋白

5’ untranslated region (5’ UTR) of bovine β-globin in the mRNA constructs translates different types of antigenic proteins
作者： Sajjanar B, Gopinath S, Nadarajan P, Marreddy V, Krishnaswamy N, Nagaleekar V, Hosamani M, Sreenivasa B, Chaudhuri P, Basagoudanavar S
摘要中文翻译合成信使 RNA (mRNA) 正在成为下一代 疫苗 平台。由于特定基因的天然 5'UTR 只能作用于其自身的 mRNA,因此有必要确定一个理想的 5'UTR,以支持不同抗原的更高翻译效率。来自 宿主 动物的 α、β-球蛋白基因和其他病毒基因的 6 个天然 5'UTR 被克隆到报告蛋白编码序列的上游。通过体外转录产生相应的加帽mRNA,并使用流式细胞术、生物发光、免疫荧光和蛋白质印迹实验评估翻译效率。结果表明,牛 β-珠蛋白 5'UTR 在不同细胞系中对报告蛋白的翻译更高。此外,所选的5'UTR支持mRNA翻译以产生不同的抗原蛋白,例如狂犬病病毒糖蛋白(RVG)、口蹄疫病毒(VP1)的主要结构蛋白和细菌ε类毒素蛋白(Eptx)。牛珠蛋白5'UTR不仅提高了报告蛋白的翻译效率,而且支持不同抗原蛋白的表达。因此,牛珠蛋白 5'UTR 可用于针对动物疾病的 mRNA 疫苗 设计。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决需要一种理想的5’UTR以支持不同抗原的更高翻译效率的问题,因为某些基因的天然5’UTR仅对其自身的mRNA有效。这对于下一代mRNA 疫苗平台的开发至关重要。
设计与对象
本研究涉及从宿主动物的α、β-珠蛋白基因以及其他病毒基因中克隆六个天然5’UTR,并将其连接到报告蛋白的编码序列上游。翻译效率在不同细胞系中进行了评估。
核心方法
通过体外转录生成相应的加帽mRNA,并使用流式细胞术、生物发光、免疫荧光和Western blot实验评估翻译效率。
主要结果
结果表明,牛β-珠蛋白5’UTR显著提高了报告蛋白在不同细胞系中的翻译效率。此外,它还支持多种抗原蛋白的表达,如狂犬病病毒糖蛋白(RVG)、口蹄疫病毒的主要结构蛋白(VP1)以及细菌epsilon毒素蛋白(Eptx)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为牛β-珠蛋白5’UTR可以有效地用于针对动物疾病的mRNA 疫苗设计的证据,突出了其在支持不同抗原蛋白翻译方面的多功能性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更有效的针对动物疾病的mRNA 疫苗提供信息,特别是针对狂犬病病毒及相关病原体的疫苗,通过提供一种可靠的5’UTR来增强翻译效率。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于体外评估,未包括体内验证,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍适用性。然而,结果得到了多种实验方法的支持,对牛β-珠蛋白5’UTR有效性的信心中等。

5’ untranslated region (5’ UTR) of bovine β-globin in the mRNA constructs translates different types of antigenic proteins

Authors: Sajjanar B, Gopinath S, Nadarajan P, Marreddy V, Krishnaswamy N, Nagaleekar V, Hosamani M, Sreenivasa B, Chaudhuri P, Basagoudanavar S
Original abstract (English)
Synthetic messenger RNA (mRNA) is emerging as next generation vaccine platform. As natural 5’UTRs of particular genes do not work for other than its own mRNA, it is essential to identify an ideal 5’UTR supporting higher translation efficiency for different antigens. Six natural 5’UTRs derived from α, β-globin genes of host animals and other viral genes were cloned upstream to the coding sequences of reporter proteins. The corresponding capped mRNA were produced through in vitro transcription and the translation efficiency was assessed using flow cytometry, bioluminescence, immunofluorescence and western blot experiments. The results revealed higher translation of reporter proteins by bovine β-globin 5’UTR in different cell lines. Further, the chosen 5’UTR supported mRNA translation to produce different antigenic proteins such as rabies virus glycoprotein (RVG), major structural protein of foot and mouth disease virus (VP1) and bacterial epsilon toxoid protein (Eptx). Bovine -globin 5’UTR not only increased the translation efficiency of reporter proteins but also supported the expression of different antigenic proteins. Hence, bovine -globin 5’UTR can be used in the mRNA vaccine designs against animal diseases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for an ideal 5’UTR that supports higher translation efficiency for different antigens, as natural 5’UTRs of particular genes do not work for other than its own mRNA. This is critical for the development of next-generation mRNA vaccine platforms.
Design and population
The study involved cloning six natural 5’UTRs from α, β-globin genes of host animals and other viral genes upstream to the coding sequences of reporter proteins. The translation efficiency was assessed in different cell lines.
Core methods
The corresponding capped mRNA were produced through in vitro transcription, and translation efficiency was evaluated using flow cytometry, bioluminescence, immunofluorescence and western blot experiments.
Main results
The results revealed that the bovine β-globin 5’UTR significantly increased the translation efficiency of reporter proteins across different cell lines. Additionally, it supported the expression of various antigenic proteins such as rabies virus glycoprotein (RVG), major structural protein of foot and mouth disease virus (VP1) and bacterial epsilon toxoid protein (Eptx).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence that the bovine β-globin 5’UTR can be effectively used in mRNA vaccine designs against animal diseases, highlighting its versatility in supporting translation of different antigenic proteins.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more effective mRNA vaccines for animal diseases, particularly those targeting rabies virus and related pathogens, by providing a reliable 5’UTR that enhances translation efficiency.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro assessments and does not include in vivo validation, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. However, the results are supported by multiple experimental methods, providing moderate confidence in the effectiveness of the bovine β-globin 5’UTR.

评估加纳人对未来传染病的准备情况 暴发:COVID-19 经验

Assessing Preparedness of Ghanaians for Future Infectious Disease Outbreaks: COVID-19 Experience
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译COVID-19 大流行暴露了准备差距,并强化了个人和家庭行为在预防传染病 传播 方面的作用。本研究利用 COVID-19 经验教训,评估了加纳成年人对未来 传播 的准备情况。采用有目的和集群的方法,对阿散蒂、大阿克拉和萨凡纳地区六个地区的 1,307 名成年人进行了一项基于社区的横断面混合方法研究抽样。通过半结构化问卷收集数据,涵盖社会人口特征、知识、态度、预防措施、获得卫生服务的机会和健康信息来源。使用综合知识、态度和实践 (KAP) 评分来衡量准备情况。使用描述性统计、多元回归和中介分析来分析定量数据,而使用演绎主题内容分析来检查定性反应。受访者表现出适度的准备程度,预防措施强于特定疾病的知识。报告的行为包括COVID-19 疫苗接种 (70%)、儿童 (91%) 和成人 (87%) 中使用 LLIN、用肥皂和水洗手 (70%)、狗用 狂犬病 疫苗接种 (94%)、暴发 期间使用口罩和避免接触病人 (60%)、生病时到医疗机构就诊 (75%) 以及保留必需品至少 72 小时(52%)。准备工作似乎与性别、居住地、获得 疫苗、LLIN 和卫生设施以及可靠的健康信息有关,这些可能会介导社会人口影响。持续存在的差距包括 疫苗 安全问题、LLIN 使用、报告异常健康事件以及获得卫生设施和预防资源。加强健康素养、可信风险沟通、社区参与和公平获得常规预防服务可能会改善为未来传染病做好准备 暴发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在借鉴 COVID-19 的经验教训,评估加纳成年人对未来传染病 暴发 的准备情况。这次大流行暴露了准备工作的差距,并强调了个人和家庭行为在预防传染病方面的作用 传播。
设计与对象
对阿散蒂、大阿克拉和萨凡纳地区六个地区的 1,307 名成年人进行了一项基于社区的横断面混合方法研究。该研究的重点是根据社会人口特征、知识、态度、预防措施、获得卫生服务的机会和健康信息来源,评估未来 暴发 的准备情况。
核心方法
使用半结构化问卷收集数据,并使用描述性统计、多元回归和中介分析对定量数据进行分析。使用演绎主题内容分析来检查定性反应。使用综合知识、态度和实践 (KAP) 分数来衡量准备情况。
主要结果
受访者表现出适度的准备,预防措施比特定疾病的知识更强。报告的行为包括 COVID-19 疫苗接种 (70%)、儿童 (91%) 和成人 (87%) 使用 LLIN、用肥皂和水洗手 (70%)、狗 狂犬病 疫苗接种 (94%)、在 暴发 期间使用口罩和避免接触病人 (60%)、生病时前往医疗机构就诊 (75%) 以及保留基本用品至少 72 小时 (52%)。
解释与创新
准备程度似乎与性别、居住地、获得 疫苗、LLIN 和卫生设施以及可靠的健康信息有关,这些可能会产生社会人口影响。持续存在的差距包括 疫苗 安全问题、LLIN 使用、异常健康事件报告以及卫生设施和预防资源的使用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可能会为加强健康素养、可信风险沟通、社区参与和公平获得常规预防服务的策略提供信息,以改善对未来传染病的准备。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于自我报告的数据,可能存在回忆偏差。证据仅是抽象的,置信度中等。

Assessing Preparedness of Ghanaians for Future Infectious Disease Outbreaks: COVID-19 Experience

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
The COVID-19 pandemic exposed preparedness gaps and reinforced the role of individual and household behaviours in preventing infectious disease transmission.This study assessed preparedness for future outbreaks among adults in Ghana, using lessons from the COVID-19 experience.A community-based cross-sectional mixed-method study was conducted among 1,307 adults in six districts across the Ashanti, Greater Accra, and Savannah regions, using purposive and cluster sampling.Data were collected with a semi-structured questionnaire covering sociodemographic characteristics, knowledge, attitudes, preventive practices, access to health services, and health information sources.Preparedness was measured using a composite knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) score.Quantitative data were analysed using descriptive statistics, multivariate regression, and mediation analysis, while qualitative responses were examined using deductive thematic content analysis.Respondents demonstrated moderate preparedness, with preventive practices stronger than disease-specific knowledge.Reported behaviours included COVID-19 vaccination (70%), LLIN use among children (91%) and adults (87%), handwashing with soap and water (70%), rabies vaccination for dogs (94%), mask use and avoidance of sick people during outbreaks (60%), health facility visits when sick (75%) and keeping essential supplies for at least 72 hours (52%).Preparedness seemed associated with sex, residence, access to vaccines, LLINs and health facilities, and credible health information, which may have mediated socio-demographic influences.Persistent gaps included vaccine safety concerns, LLIN use, reporting of unusual health events, and access to hygiene facilities and preventive resources.Strengthening health literacy, trusted risk communication, community engagement, and equitable access to routine preventive services may improve preparedness for future infectious disease outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to assess preparedness for future infectious disease outbreaks among adults in Ghana, using lessons from the COVID-19 experience. The pandemic exposed preparedness gaps and highlighted the role of individual and household behaviors in preventing infectious disease transmission.
Design and population
A community-based cross-sectional mixed-method study was conducted among 1,307 adults in six districts across the Ashanti, Greater Accra, and Savannah regions. The study focused on assessing preparedness for future outbreaks based on sociodemographic characteristics, knowledge, attitudes, preventive practices, access to health services, and health information sources.
Core methods
Data were collected using a semi-structured questionnaire and analyzed with descriptive statistics, multivariate regression, and mediation analysis for quantitative data. Qualitative responses were examined using deductive thematic content analysis. Preparedness was measured using a composite knowledge, attitudes, and practices (KAP) score.
Main results
Respondents demonstrated moderate preparedness, with preventive practices stronger than disease-specific knowledge. Reported behaviors included COVID-19 vaccination (70%), LLIN use among children (91%) and adults (87%), handwashing with soap and water (70%), rabies vaccination for dogs (94%), mask use and avoidance of sick people during outbreaks (60%), health facility visits when sick (75%) and keeping essential supplies for at least 72 hours (52%).
Interpretation and novelty
Preparedness seemed associated with sex, residence, access to vaccines, LLINs and health facilities, and credible health information, which may have mediated socio-demographic influences. Persistent gaps included vaccine safety concerns, LLIN use, reporting of unusual health events, and access to hygiene facilities and preventive resources.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform strategies to strengthen health literacy, trusted risk communication, community engagement, and equitable access to routine preventive services for improving preparedness for future infectious disease outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on self-reported data and may be subject to recall bias. The evidence is abstract-only, and the confidence level is moderate.

动物咬伤病例的临床和社会人口学概况:印度东部的前瞻性观察研究

Clinical and Sociodemographic Profile of Animal Bite Cases: Prospective Observational Study in Eastern India
作者： Rupak Chatterjee, Subhajit Dey Sarkar, Shatavisa Mukherjee, Susmita Roy, Atreyi C Banerjee, Chitra Barick, Nandini Chatterjee
摘要中文翻译背景:动物咬伤是全球一个重要的公共卫生问题,流行病学 的发生率很高,尤其是在印度等国家。狗咬伤(主要来自流浪狗)占大多数病例。虽然感染很常见,但 狂犬病 仍然是最严重且可预防的并发症。本研究重点关注印度西孟加拉邦南 24 帕尔加纳斯的 流行病学、管理和与动物咬伤相关的并发症。方法:前瞻性在政府卫生机构进行了为期 2 年(2022 年 1 月至 2023 年 12 月)的观察性研究。所有寻求动物咬伤暴露后预防的患者都被纳入其中,不包括未知动物的咬伤。通过结构化调查问卷收集数据,包括人口统计详细信息、咬伤细节和临床检查。随访 1 个月,以监测免疫接种后的不良反应 (AEFI)。使用描述性统计分析数据。结果:总共共纳入 2,888 名患者,其中男性占 60%,主要年龄层为 21-30 岁。大多数咬伤是由家庭周围/流浪动物咬伤(63.85%),77.49%是无端咬伤。大多数咬伤属于 III 类(53%),下肢是最常见的受影响部位。而 94.22% 的人完成了抗狂犬病治疗疫苗接种 (ARV) 病程中,1% 的病例出现发烧和迟发性皮肤过敏等轻度 AEFI。结论:动物咬伤是一个严重的公共卫生问题,其中流浪狗是主要来源。遵守 疫苗接种 方案对于预防 狂犬病 至关重要,并且 ARV 引起的轻微副作用很少见。公众认识和遵守国家管理指南对于减少 疫苗接种 的影响至关重要。这些伤害。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
动物咬伤是全球重大的公共卫生问题,发病率 较高,尤其是在印度等国家。狂犬病 仍然是这些伤害中最严重且可预防的并发症。
设计与对象
一项前瞻性观察性研究在印度西孟加拉邦南24区的政府卫生机构进行,持续时间为2年(2022年1月至2023年12月)。所有因动物咬伤寻求暴露后预防的患者均被纳入研究,排除了来自未知动物的咬伤。共纳入2,888名患者,其中60%为男性,主要年龄组为21-30岁。
核心方法
通过结构化调查问卷收集数据,包括人口统计详细信息、咬合细节和临床检查。随访 1 个月,以监测免疫后的不良反应 (AEFI)。使用描述性统计分析数据。
主要结果
大多数咬伤是由家养/流浪动物咬伤(63.85%),77.49%是无端咬伤。大多数咬伤属于 III 类(53%),下肢是最常受影响的部位。虽然 94.22% 的人完成了抗狂犬病 疫苗接种 (ARV) 疗程,但有 1% 的病例观察到轻度 AEFI,例如发烧和迟发性皮肤超敏反应。
解释与创新
动物咬伤是一个重要的公共卫生问题,其中流浪狗是主要来源。遵守 疫苗接种 方案对于预防 狂犬病 至关重要,并且抗逆转录病毒药物产生的轻微副作用很少见。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了公众意识和遵守国家动物咬伤管理指南的重要性,以减少这些伤害的影响。他们还强调需要改进高危人群的 狂犬病 预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于印度西孟加拉邦南 24 帕尔加纳斯的一个政府卫生机构,这可能会影响普遍性。证据是描述性的并且基于单一中心,因此置信度中等。

Clinical and Sociodemographic Profile of Animal Bite Cases: Prospective Observational Study in Eastern India

Authors: Rupak Chatterjee, Subhajit Dey Sarkar, Shatavisa Mukherjee, Susmita Roy, Atreyi C Banerjee, Chitra Barick, Nandini Chatterjee
Original abstract (English)
Background: Animal bite injuries are a significant public health issue globally, with a high incidence, particularly in countries like India.Dog bites, primarily from stray dogs, account for the majority of cases.While infections are common, rabies remains the most serious and preventable complication.This study focuses on the epidemiology, management, and complications associated with animal bites in South 24 Parganas, West Bengal, India.Methods: A prospective observational study was conducted over 2 years (January 2022-December 2023) at a government health facility.All patients seeking postexposure prophylaxis for animal bites were included, excluding bites from unknown animals.Data were collected through a structured questionnaire, including demographic details, bite specifics, and clinical examination.Follow-up was done for 1 month to monitor adverse effects following immunization (AEFI).Data were analyzed using descriptive statistics.Results: A total of 2,888 patients were included, with 60% males and a predominant age-group of 21-30 years.Most bites were from peridomestic/ stray animals (63.85%), and 77.49% were unprovoked.The majority of bites were categorized as category III (53%), and the lower limb was the most commonly affected site.While 94.22% completed the antirabies vaccination (ARV) course, mild AEFIs, such as fever and delayed skin hypersensitivity, were observed in 1% of cases.Conclusion: Animal bites are a critical public health issue, with stray dogs being the main source.Adherence to vaccination protocols is crucial for preventing rabies, and mild side effects from ARVs are rare.Public awareness and adherence to national guidelines for management are essential to reduce the impact of these injuries.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Animal bite injuries are a significant public health issue globally, with a high incidence, particularly in countries like India. Rabies remains the most serious and preventable complication of these injuries.
Design and population
A prospective observational study was conducted over 2 years (January 2022-December 2023) at a government health facility in South 24 Parganas, West Bengal, India. All patients seeking postexposure prophylaxis for animal bites were included, excluding bites from unknown animals. A total of 2,888 patients were included, with 60% males and a predominant age-group of 21-30 years.
Core methods
Data were collected through a structured questionnaire, including demographic details, bite specifics, and clinical examination. Follow-up was done for 1 month to monitor adverse effects following immunization (AEFI). Data were analyzed using descriptive statistics.
Main results
Most bites were from peridomestic/stray animals (63.85%), and 77.49% were unprovoked. The majority of bites were categorized as category III (53%), and the lower limb was the most commonly affected site. While 94.22% completed the antirabies vaccination (ARV) course, mild AEFIs, such as fever and delayed skin hypersensitivity, were observed in 1% of cases.
Interpretation and novelty
Animal bites are a critical public health issue, with stray dogs being the main source. Adherence to vaccination protocols is crucial for preventing rabies, and mild side effects from ARVs are rare.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of public awareness and adherence to national guidelines for the management of animal bites to reduce the impact of these injuries. They also emphasize the need for improved rabies prevention strategies in high-risk populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single government health facility in South 24 Parganas, West Bengal, India, which may affect generalisability. The evidence is descriptive and based on a single centre, so the confidence is moderate.

深入了解 狂犬病 流行环境中扑杀狗的规模、组织、实施和动机

Insights into the scale, organisation, implementation and motivations behind dog culling in a rabies-endemic setting
作者： tasmin alexander, Kennedy Lushasi, Lwitiko Sikana, Anna Czupryna, Elaine Ferguson, Mirava Yuson, Deborah Nadal, Nicholas Hanley, Nai Rui Chng, Sarah Cleaveland
摘要中文翻译扑杀被认为对于可持续犬只数量管理和 狂犬病 控制来说是无效的,并且可能适得其反,但这种做法在许多国家仍然存在。关于扑杀背后的规模、组织、实施和动机的经验数据仍然很少,限制了对其持久性和影响的理解。本研究以坦桑尼亚为重点,该国是 狂犬病 流行的国家,有扑杀记录,本研究采用混合方法,结合了两项结构化调查(发生情况调查,n = 51;报告扑杀地区实践调查,n = 16)以及对地区和实地兽医和畜牧官员的深入访谈(n = 8)。45% 的调查地区报告了扑杀行为 (23/51)。据报道,扑杀活动是由兽医部门领导的,通常还有其他部门和社区的参与,官员们还称社区独立进行了一些扑杀活动。扑杀的动机是对自由流浪狗的数量和伤害的投诉、请求和命令。无论所有权、绝育情况和 疫苗接种 状态如何,流浪狗都被扑杀,使用的方法不符合人道宰杀标准。根据调查数据,使用随机模拟,我们估计全国每年约有 26,000 只狗(95% CI 20,000-33,000)被扑杀。扑杀很难与基于证据的犬类种群管理和 狂犬病 控制国际指南、国家动物福利立法和“同一个健康”原则相一致,这强调了了解和解决维持扑杀的因素的必要性。解决扑杀问题需要更广泛的关注,以及相关部门和社区的参与。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究调查了 狂犬病 流行环境中扑杀做法的持续性,尽管它们对于可持续犬只种群管理和 狂犬病 控制无效且可能适得其反。这种做法仍然很普遍,但有关其规模、组织和动机的经验数据有限。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注坦桑尼亚,这是一个 狂犬病 流行国家,那里有扑杀记录。它采用混合方法,结合了两次结构化调查(n = 51 和 n = 16)以及对兽医和畜牧官员的深入访谈(n = 8)。
核心方法
该研究采用混合方法设计,包括结构化调查和访谈,并采用随机模拟从调查数据推断年度扑杀估计。
主要结果
45% 的调查地区报告了扑杀行为 (23/51)。该研究还估计,全国每年约有 26,000 只狗(95% CI 20,000-33,000)被扑杀。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,扑杀是由社区投诉和机构命令驱动的,并且无论 疫苗接种 或绝育状态如何,扑杀都会发生,这凸显了实践与基于证据的指南之间的脱节。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调需要使扑杀实践与科学和公共卫生原则保持一致,并表明理解和解决扑杀背后的动机对于有效的 狂犬病 控制至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于调查数据和外推法,这可能会引入偏差并限制扑杀估计的精度。由于依赖自我报告的数据并且缺乏直接的生物学或临床测量,证据的强度中等。

Insights into the scale, organisation, implementation and motivations behind dog culling in a rabies-endemic setting

Authors: tasmin alexander, Kennedy Lushasi, Lwitiko Sikana, Anna Czupryna, Elaine Ferguson, Mirava Yuson, Deborah Nadal, Nicholas Hanley, Nai Rui Chng, Sarah Cleaveland
Original abstract (English)
Culling is recognised as ineffective and potentially counterproductive for sustainable dog population management and rabies control, yet the practice persists in many countries. Empirical data on the scale, organisation, implementation and motivations behind culling remain sparse, limiting understanding of its persistence, and implications. Focusing on Tanzania, a rabies-endemic country where culling has been documented, this study uses a mixed-methods approach, combining two structured surveys (survey of occurrence, n = 51; survey of practice in districts reporting culling, n = 16) and in-depth interviews with district and field-based veterinary and livestock officers (n = 8). Culling was reported in 45% of surveyed districts (23/51). Culling events were reportedly led by veterinary departments, often with involvement of other sectors and communities, with officers also describing communities as carrying out some culling independently. Culling was motivated by complaints, requests and orders about the number of and harms attributed to free-roaming dogs. Roaming dogs were culled regardless of ownership, sterilisation and vaccination status, using methods inconsistent with humane killing standards. Extrapolating from the survey data, using a stochastic simulation, we estimate approximately 26,000 dogs (95% CI 20,000-33,000) are culled annually nationwide. Culling was difficult to reconcile with evidence-based international guidance on dog population management and rabies control, national animal welfare legislation, and One Health principles, underscoring the need to understand and address the factors sustaining it. Addressing culling will require engaging with the wider concerns motivating the practice, and with the sectors and communities involved.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study investigates the persistence of culling practices in rabies-endemic settings despite their ineffectiveness and potential counterproductivity for sustainable dog population management and rabies control. The practice remains widespread, yet empirical data on its scale, organisation, and motivations are limited.
Design and population
The study focuses on Tanzania, a rabies-endemic country where culling has been documented. It employs a mixed-methods approach, combining two structured surveys (n = 51 and n = 16) and in-depth interviews with veterinary and livestock officers (n = 8).
Core methods
The study uses a mixed-methods design, including structured surveys and interviews, and employs a stochastic simulation to extrapolate annual culling estimates from the survey data.
Main results
Culling was reported in 45% of surveyed districts (23/51). The study also estimates that approximately 26,000 dogs (95% CI 20,000-33,000) are culled annually nationwide.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal that culling is driven by community complaints and institutional orders, and that it occurs regardless of vaccination or sterilisation status, highlighting a disconnect between practice and evidence-based guidelines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results underscore the need to align culling practices with scientific and public health principles, and suggest that understanding and addressing the motivations behind culling is critical for effective rabies control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on survey data and extrapolation, which may introduce bias and limit the precision of culling estimates. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the reliance on self-reported data and the lack of direct biological or clinical measurements.

国家考古学的古流行病学:El caso de La Mancha Alta(伊比利亚半岛)和 Edad Media

Paleoepidemiología para la Arqueología del Paisaje: el caso de La Mancha Alta (Península Ibérica) en la Edad Media
作者： Leonor Parra-Aguilar
摘要中文翻译这项研究分析了西班牙中世纪、十一世纪和十六世纪期间经常记录的有利于疾病发生的环境条件,例如 狂犬病、麦角中毒、鼠疫和疟疾。由于这些疾病需要特定的环境条件才能出现和传播,因此可以将它们作为过去气候条件的间接指标进行探索。结果表明,狂犬病 和麦角中毒在中世纪温暖时期更为普遍,当时的环境条件有利于它们的 传播。相比之下,鼠疫和疟疾在十四世纪和十五世纪广泛传播,恰逢气候向小冰河时期的转变,即中世纪晚期气候转变。这些疾病对研究区的人口产生了重大影响,为古环境重建提供了宝贵的证据。观察到的疾病发生与气候条件之间的关系表明,中世纪温暖期的特点是温暖和潮湿的条件,中世纪晚期气候转变的特点是温暖和干燥的条件,小冰河时期的特点是寒冷和干燥的条件。

Paleoepidemiología para la Arqueología del Paisaje: el caso de La Mancha Alta (Península Ibérica) en la Edad Media

Authors: Leonor Parra-Aguilar
Original abstract (English)
This study analyses the environmental conditions that favored the occurrence of diseases frequently documented during the Spanish Middle Ages, the eleventh and sixteenth centuries, such as rabies, ergotism, plague, and malaria. Because these diseases require specific environmental conditions to emerge and spread, they can be explored as indirect indicators of past climatic conditions. The results suggest that rabies and ergotism were more prevalent during the Medieval Warm Period, when environmental conditions favored their transmission. In contrast, plague and malaria became widespread during the fourteenth and fifteenth centuries, coinciding with the climatic shift toward the Little Ice Age known as the Late Medieval Climate Transition. These diseases had a significant impact on the populations of the study area and provide valuable evidence for palaeoenvironmental reconstruction. The observed relationships between disease occurrence and climatic conditions suggest that the Medieval Warm Period was characterized by warm and humid conditions, the Late Medieval Climate Transition by warm and dry conditions, and the onset of the Little Ice Age by cold and dry conditions.

Design and population

Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Limitations and evidence strength

单细胞转录组学和抗体免疫治疗:对抗抗疟药物耐药性的潜在双重策略

Single-cell Transcriptomics and Antibody Immunotherapy: A Potential Dual Strategy against Antimalarial Drug Resistance
作者： Leo Nnamdi Ozurumba-Dwight
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了抗疟药物耐药性的挑战,这仍然是疟疾控制工作的一个主要障碍,并探讨了单细胞转录组学和抗体免疫疗法作为解决这一问题的双重策略的潜力。
设计与对象
该研究提出了一个将单细胞 RNA 测序与抗体免疫疗法相结合的概念框架,重点关注恶性疟原虫和相关物种,并概述了这种方法识别和靶向耐药寄生虫亚群的潜力。
核心方法
这些方法包括使用单细胞 RNA 测序来绘制转录异质性并识别特定阶段的漏洞,结合这些转录组学见解提供的抗体免疫疗法,并利用机器学习和人工智能来指导靶点选择。
主要结果
该研究报告乌干达恶性疟原虫分离株对二氢叶酸还原酶抑制剂的敏感性降低,并强调表观遗传学赋予青蒿素 治疗 的环期存活率。
解释与创新
作者解释说,单细胞转录组学可以帮助识别寄生虫阶段的漏洞并指导未来的目标发现,而基于抗体的预防和其他精准干预措施需要进一步验证可负担性、交付、耐久性和临床影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将单细胞转录组学与抗体免疫疗法相结合有助于减少耐药克隆的出现,并减少疟疾的复发和 传播 特定阶段。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认采样和细胞回收偏差等局限性,以及将转录组特征转化为临床可操作目标的挑战,并指出由于该框架的概念性质,证据是温和的。

Malaria remains a serious global health challenge, with 282 million cases and 610,000 deaths in 2024.1 Despite gains from artemisinin-based combination therapies, drug resistance remains a major challenge in malaria control efforts and elimination,2,3 with Egypt certified malaria-free by WHO in 2024.1 Notably,...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the challenge of antimalarial drug resistance, which remains a major obstacle in malaria control efforts, and explores the potential of single-cell transcriptomics and antibody immunotherapy as a dual strategy to combat this issue.
Design and population
The study presents a conceptual framework combining single-cell RNA sequencing with antibody immunotherapy, focusing on Plasmodium falciparum and related species, and outlines the potential for this approach to identify and target drug-resistant parasite subpopulations.
Core methods
The methods involve using single-cell RNA sequencing to map transcriptional heterogeneity and identify stage-specific vulnerabilities, combined with antibody immunotherapy informed by these transcriptomic insights, and leveraging machine learning and artificial intelligence to guide target selection.
Main results
The study reports decreased susceptibility to dihydrofolate reductase inhibitors in Ugandan Plasmodium falciparum isolates and highlights epigenetically conferred ring-stage survival against artemisinin treatment.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that single-cell transcriptomics can help identify parasite-stage vulnerabilities and guide future target discovery, while antibody-based prevention and other precision interventions require further validation for affordability, delivery, durability, and clinical impact.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that integrating single-cell transcriptomics with antibody immunotherapy could help mitigate the emergence of drug-resistant clones and reduce recrudescence and transmission-specific stages of malaria.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges limitations such as sampling and cell-recovery biases, as well as challenges in translating transcriptomic signatures into clinically actionable targets, and notes that the evidence is moderate due to the conceptual nature of the framework.

狂犬病 是人类历史上最早记录的人畜共患疾病之一。尽管科学取得了几个世纪的进步,但由于其严重且进展迅速的性质,它仍然是最令人恐惧的传染病之一。

对撒哈拉以南非洲野生食肉动物 狂犬病 控制策略的叙述性回顾

A narrative review of rabies control strategies in wild carnivores across sub-Saharan Africa
作者： Phoebe Strawford-Sussmann, Nicola Rooney, Irene Bueno, Laurie Marker, Anne Schmidt-Küntzel, Anne Schmidt‐Küntzel
摘要中文翻译狂犬病 是一种致命的人畜共患疾病,每年导致约 59,000 人死亡、重大牲畜损失以及濒临灭绝的野生动物种群。豺狼(Lupulella adusta 和 Lupulella mesomelas)、蝙蝠耳狐(Otocyon megalotis)和猫鼬(Cynictis penicillata 和 Herpestes sanguineus)已被确定为撒哈拉以南非洲地区潜在的维持动物 宿主。本综述旨在确定最有效的控制(考虑预防措施、人口控制和疫苗接种)和疾病监测策略,为整个地区实施狂犬病控制计划提供指导。我们进行了叙述性审查,以查找有关全球野生食肉动物(特别是撒哈拉以南非洲地区)狂犬病 控制策略的相关同行评审文章。总的来说,我们的结论是需要多因素控制策略。证据表明,需要将预防措施和点感染控制与同时的 疫苗接种 相结合,以控制初始 狂犬病 疫苗接种,随后对野生食肉动物进行大规模口服 疫苗接种,对濒危人群进行肠胃外 疫苗接种(口服以外的途径,例如肌内和皮下注射),并优化大规模狗 疫苗接种 活动,以实现长期狂犬病控制。这些需要通过强大的 监测 网络收集的数据来了解。我们的审查很少有针对撒哈拉以南非洲地区的控制策略实施的现有研究和报告。因此,控制撒哈拉以南非洲野生食肉动物中的 狂犬病 将需要大量的研究、资源、有效和综合的 狂犬病 系统以及跨学科合作,以在当地和区域范围内制定和实施控制策略。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
我们的审查很少有针对撒哈拉以南非洲地区的控制策略实施的现有研究和报告。
证据基础与方法
我们进行了叙述性审查,以查找有关全球野生食肉动物(特别是撒哈拉以南非洲地区)狂犬病 控制策略的相关同行评审文章。
共识与结论
总的来说,我们的结论是需要多因素控制策略。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据没有明确指出争议或证据差距。
科研与实践启示
因此,控制撒哈拉以南非洲野生食肉动物中的 狂犬病 将需要大量的研究、资源、有效和综合的 狂犬病 系统以及跨学科合作,以在当地和区域范围内制定和实施控制策略。

A narrative review of rabies control strategies in wild carnivores across sub-Saharan Africa

Authors: Phoebe Strawford-Sussmann, Nicola Rooney, Irene Bueno, Laurie Marker, Anne Schmidt-Küntzel, Anne Schmidt‐Küntzel
Original abstract (English)
Rabies is a fatal zoonotic disease responsible for approximately 59,000 human deaths each year, significant livestock losses, and mortality in endangered wildlife populations. Jackals (Lupulella adusta and Lupulella mesomelas), bat-eared foxes (Otocyon megalotis) and mongooses (Cynictis penicillata and Herpestes sanguineus) have been identified as potential maintenance hosts in sub-Saharan Africa. This review aims to identify the most effective control (considering preventive measures, population control, and vaccination) and disease surveillance strategies to provide a guide for the implementation of rabies control plans across the region. A narrative review was conducted to find relevant peer-reviewed articles surrounding rabies control strategies within wild carnivores across the globe and specifically within sub-Saharan Africa. Overall, we conclude that a multifactorial control strategy is needed. The evidence suggests that a combination of preventive measures and point infection control with concurrent vaccination will be required to control initial rabies outbreaks, followed by mass oral vaccination of wild carnivores, parenteral vaccination (routes other than oral, such as intramuscular and subcutaneous injection) of endangered populations and optimisation of mass dog vaccination campaigns to enable long-term rabies control. These will need to be informed by data collected through strong surveillance networks. Our review yielded few existing studies and reports of control strategy implementation focused on sub-Saharan Africa. Therefore, controlling rabies within wild carnivores across sub-Saharan Africa will require considerable research, resources, effective and integrated surveillance systems, and interdisciplinary collaboration to develop and implement control strategies at a local and regional scale.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Our review yielded few existing studies and reports of control strategy implementation focused on sub-Saharan Africa.
Evidence base and method
A narrative review was conducted to find relevant peer-reviewed articles surrounding rabies control strategies within wild carnivores across the globe and specifically within sub-Saharan Africa.
Consensus and conclusions
Overall, we conclude that a multifactorial control strategy is needed.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
Therefore, controlling rabies within wild carnivores across sub-Saharan Africa will require considerable research, resources, effective and integrated surveillance systems, and interdisciplinary collaboration to develop and implement control strategies at a local and regional scale.

2025 年东帝汶马努法希市 狂犬病 监测 和狗咬伤管理情况分析:混合方法质量数据审计

Situation Analysis of Rabies Surveillance and Dog Bite Management in Manufahi Municipality of Timor-Leste, 2025: A Mixed-Methods Quality Data Audit
作者： Quintão De Andrade, Cristovão de Alexandria Barros, Orlando Martins, Erti Fatima Fernandes, Claudio De Jesus Oliveira, Domingos Soares
摘要中文翻译目标:狂犬病 已成为东帝汶的重大公共卫生威胁,自该疾病于 2024 年初首次出现以来,已有 24 人死亡。虽然该病毒最初集中在边境地区,但已传播到包括马努法希市在内的多个城市。本研究对 2025 年马努法希市的狗咬伤管理情况进行了情况分析,涉及 狂犬病 风险、监测 报告的及时性以及运营因素。方法:对四个社区卫生中心采用了混合方法质量数据审计。数据收集包括对临床区域的直接观察、对 17 名基本卫生人员的半结构化访谈以及对门诊和实验室登记簿的系统审查和交叉验证。结果:监测系统周报及时性达到100%。2025 年,马努法希市记录了 295 起狗咬伤案件,对应的 发病率 为每 1,000 人 4.63 起。两名患者 (0.7%) 死亡,其临床症状与 狂犬病 一致。5至14岁儿童的负担最高,占病例的52.9%。虽然 疫苗 于 2025 年 6 月到达,但第一剂抗 疫苗 (ARV) 总体覆盖率达到 84.4% (249/295),合格的 II 类和 III 类暴露覆盖率达到 87.7% (249/284)。然而,多剂量依从性下降至 28.9%(2 剂;82/284)和 7.0%(3 剂;20/284)。高危III类病例中狂犬病免疫球蛋白(RIG)覆盖率为13.7%(31/226)。公共卫生建议:改善 狂犬病 控制需要标准化报告格式并优先考虑 监测 系统的数字化。采用“同一个健康”方法并增强社区层面的 疫苗 可及性对于可持续消除疾病至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
狂犬病 已成为东帝汶的重大公共卫生威胁,自该疾病于 2024 年初首次出现以来,已有 24 人死亡。虽然该病毒最初集中在边境地区,但现已传播到包括马努法希市在内的多个城市。
证据基础与方法
四个社区卫生中心采用了混合方法质量数据审计。数据收集包括对临床区域的直接观察、对 17 名基本卫生人员的半结构化访谈以及对门诊和实验室登记簿的系统审查和交叉验证。
共识与结论
监测系统周报及时性达到100%。2025 年,马努法希市记录了 295 起狗咬伤案件,对应的 发病率 为每 1,000 人 4.63 起。两名患者 (0.7%) 死亡,其临床症状与 狂犬病 一致。5至14岁儿童的负担最高,占病例的52.9%。虽然 疫苗 于 2025 年 6 月到达,但第一剂抗 疫苗 (ARV) 总体覆盖率达到 84.4% (249/295),合格的 II 类和 III 类暴露覆盖率达到 87.7% (249/284)。高危III类病例中狂犬病免疫球蛋白(RIG)覆盖率为13.7%(31/226)。
争议与缺口
然而,多剂量依从性下降至 28.9%(2 剂;82/284)和 7.0%(3 剂;20/284)。
科研与实践启示
公共卫生建议:改善 狂犬病 控制需要标准化报告格式并优先考虑 监测 系统的数字化。采用“同一个健康”方法并增强社区层面的 疫苗 可及性对于可持续消除疾病至关重要。

Situation Analysis of Rabies Surveillance and Dog Bite Management in Manufahi Municipality of Timor-Leste, 2025: A Mixed-Methods Quality Data Audit

Authors: Quintão De Andrade, Cristovão de Alexandria Barros, Orlando Martins, Erti Fatima Fernandes, Claudio De Jesus Oliveira, Domingos Soares
Original abstract (English)
Objectives: Rabies has emerged as a significant public health threat in Timor-Leste, with 24 recorded human deaths since the disease first appeared in early 2024. While initially concentrated in border areas, the virus has spread to multiple municipalities, including Manufahi Municipality. This study presents a situational analysis of dog bite management concerning rabies risk, the timeliness of surveillance reports, and operational factors in Manufahi Municipality during 2025. Methods: A mixed-methods quality data audit was employed across four community health centers. Data collection encompassed direct observations of clinical areas, semi-structured interviews with 17 essential health personnel, and a methodical review and cross-verification of outpatient and laboratory register books. Results: The surveillance system achieved 100% timeliness in weekly reporting. In 2025, Manufahi Municipality recorded 295 dog bite cases, corresponding to an incidence of 4.63 per 1,000 population. Two patients (0.7%) died with clinical signs consistent with rabies. Children aged 5 to 14 years had the highest burden, accounting for 52.9% of cases. While vaccines arrived in June 2025, first-dose anti-rabies vaccine (ARV) coverage reached 84.4% overall (249/295) and 87.7% among eligible category II and III exposures (249/284). However, multi-dose adherence dropped to 28.9% (2 doses; 82/284) and 7.0% (3 doses; 20/284). Rabies immune globulin (RIG) coverage was 13.7% (31/226) among high-risk category III cases. Public Health Recommendations: Improving rabies control requires standardizing reporting formats and prioritizing the digitalization of surveillance systems. Adopting a "One Health" approach and enhancing community-level vaccine accessibility are vital for sustainable disease elimination.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Rabies has emerged as a significant public health threat in Timor-Leste, with 24 recorded human deaths since the disease first appeared in early 2024. While initially concentrated in border areas, the virus has spread to multiple municipalities, including Manufahi Municipality.
Evidence base and method
A mixed-methods quality data audit was employed across four community health centers. Data collection encompassed direct observations of clinical areas, semi-structured interviews with 17 essential health personnel, and a methodical review and cross-verification of outpatient and laboratory register books.
Consensus and conclusions
The surveillance system achieved 100% timeliness in weekly reporting. In 2025, Manufahi Municipality recorded 295 dog bite cases, corresponding to an incidence of 4.63 per 1,000 population. Two patients (0.7%) died with clinical signs consistent with rabies. Children aged 5 to 14 years had the highest burden, accounting for 52.9% of cases. While vaccines arrived in June 2025, first-dose anti-rabies vaccine (ARV) coverage reached 84.4% overall (249/295) and 87.7% among eligible category II and III exposures (249/284). Rabies immune globulin (RIG) coverage was 13.7% (31/226) among high-risk category III cases.
Controversies and gaps
However, multi-dose adherence dropped to 28.9% (2 doses; 82/284) and 7.0% (3 doses; 20/284).
Research and practice implications
Public Health Recommendations: Improving rabies control requires standardizing reporting formats and prioritizing the digitalization of surveillance systems. Adopting a "One Health" approach and enhancing community-level vaccine accessibility are vital for sustainable disease elimination.

从视网膜到大脑:针对视网膜细胞和绘制视觉回路的病毒工具。

From Retina to Brain: Viral Tools for Targeting Retinal Cells and Mapping Visual Circuits.
作者： Venu Talla, Kevin K. Park
摘要中文翻译视网膜是中枢神经系统的独特入口点,可实现细胞类型特异性操作和视觉通路的电路级询问。病毒 媒介 已成为这种背景下不可或缺的工具,促进精确的基因传递、功能扰动以及视网膜和大脑回路的连接映射。在视网膜神经元中,视网膜神经节细胞(RGC)因其作为向中央目标传递视觉信息的唯一输出神经元的作用而成为主要焦点。因此,病毒工程的最新进展,包括衣壳设计、转录靶向和合成调控元件,显着提高了 RGC 引导的基因递送的效率和特异性,同时还能够靶向不同的视网膜细胞群。除了视网膜靶向之外,病毒工具还改变了 RGC 与下游脑核连接的研究。特别是,跨突触和投射特异性病毒策略现在能够以越来越高的精度映射视觉通路上的 RGC 输入和输出。使用逆行 AAV、单突触 狂犬病 系统和顺行示踪剂的方法揭示了连接视网膜与丘脑、中脑和皮质回路的复杂的、依赖于细胞类型的接线模式。在这篇综述中,我们重点介绍了当前针对 RGC 和其他视网膜细胞类型的病毒技术,并随后讨论了解剖视网膜-大脑连接的病毒方法。这些互补策略共同提供了一个强大的框架,用于连接分子身份、电路结构和视觉功能,从而推进基础神经科学和视觉研究的转化工作。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
所提供的证据围绕用于视网膜细胞靶向和视网膜脑回路映射的病毒工具进行了审查,特定目标重点关注单突触 狂犬病 系统作为询问视网膜神经节细胞连接性的方法。
证据基础与方法
提供的证据不报告数据库、检索日期、资格标准、研究选择方法、纳入研究计数、荟萃分析或正式共识过程;它将这篇文章视为一篇综述,强调了当前的病毒技术,并讨论了解剖视网膜-大脑连接的病毒方法。
共识与结论
单突触 狂犬病 系统与逆行 AAV 和顺行示踪剂一起揭示了连接视网膜与丘脑、中脑和皮质回路的复杂的、依赖于细胞类型的接线模式。
争议与缺口
所提供的证据未报告异质性、相互矛盾的发现、薄弱的方法、缺失的人群或区域、未解决的机制或特定于 狂犬病病毒 的具体证据差距;它只是指出这些方法揭示了复杂的布线模式。
科研与实践启示
提供的证据未报告 狂犬病病毒 研究、监测、诊断、治疗、疫苗接种、生态、风险评估或政策的具体优先事项;它仅表明互补的病毒策略推进了基础神经科学和转化视觉研究。

From Retina to Brain: Viral Tools for Targeting Retinal Cells and Mapping Visual Circuits.

Authors: Venu Talla, Kevin K. Park
Original abstract (English)
The retina serves as a uniquely accessible entry point to the central nervous system, enabling both cell type-specific manipulation and circuit-level interrogation of visual pathways. Viral vectors have emerged as indispensable tools in this context, facilitating precise gene delivery, functional perturbation, and connectivity mapping across retinal and brain circuits. Among retinal neurons, retinal ganglion cells (RGCs) represent a major focus due to their role as the sole output neurons conveying visual information to central targets. Accordingly, recent advances in viral engineering including capsid design, transcriptional targeting, and synthetic regulatory elements have significantly improved the efficiency and specificity of RGC-directed gene delivery, while also enabling targeting of diverse retinal cell populations. Beyond retinal targeting, viral tools have transformed the study of RGC connectivity with downstream brain nuclei. In particular, transsynaptic and projection-specific viral strategies now enable mapping of RGC inputs and outputs across visual pathways with increasing precision. Approaches using retrograde AAVs, monosynaptic rabies systems, and anterograde tracers have revealed complex, cell type-dependent wiring patterns linking the retina to thalamic, midbrain, and cortical circuits. In this review, we highlight current viral technologies for targeting RGCs and other retinal cell types and subsequently discuss viral approaches for dissecting retinal-brain connectivity. Together, these complementary strategies provide a powerful framework for linking molecular identity, circuit architecture, and visual function, thereby advancing both basic neuroscience and translational efforts in vision research.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The supplied evidence frames the review around viral tools for retinal cell targeting and retinal-brain circuit mapping, with the target-specific focus on monosynaptic rabies systems as a method for interrogating retinal ganglion cell connectivity.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not report databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, study selection methods, included-study counts, meta-analysis, or a formal consensus process; it identifies the article as a review that highlights current viral technologies and discusses viral approaches for dissecting retinal-brain connectivity.
Consensus and conclusions
Monosynaptic rabies systems, together with retrograde AAVs and anterograde tracers, have revealed complex, cell type-dependent wiring patterns linking the retina to thalamic, midbrain, and cortical circuits.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not report heterogeneity, conflicting findings, weak methods, missing populations or regions, unresolved mechanisms, or concrete evidence gaps specific to rabies virus; it only states that these approaches have revealed complex wiring patterns.
Research and practice implications
The supplied evidence does not report specific priorities for rabies virus research, surveillance, diagnosis, treatment, vaccination, ecology, risk assessment, or policy; it only indicates that complementary viral strategies advance basic neuroscience and translational vision research.
